Theorem 229.1 (Extension Principle)

Statement: For any logic module M satisfying the Extension Principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 137. The projective resolution has length at most 389 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the Extension Principle decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_137(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 137. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^389(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 229.2 (Extension Principle)

Statement: For Extension Principle pairs (A,B) in logic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 389 and the invariant I(A,B) = 593 mod 3.

Proof: Consider the Extension Principle operator L on the logic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 389 for all k. By the Extension Principle theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 593. QED.
